∎

D.3
Minimal
CPL

To obtain a minimal system we cannot take as atoms the “classical” atoms
i_{a}
and
\lnot i_{a}
. For simplicity, we can directly take as atoms the constants

\mathsf{Flip}_{i}
from
\lambda\textsf{{CPL}}
.

The formulas
A,B
of
mCPL
are defined by the grammar below:

\displaystyle A_{0},B_{0}=\mathsf{Flip}_{i}\mid A\Rightarrow B_{0}\qquad A,B=\mathbf{C}^{q}A_{0}

Observe that, as in
\lambda\textsf{{CPL}}
, counting quantifiers are not named.

An
intuitionistic labelled sequent
is of the form
\Delta\vdash^{X}b\rightarrowtail A
, where
\Delta
is a finite multiset of
non-labelled
formulas and
\mathrm{FN}(b)\subseteq X
.
The rules of the system
mCPL
are shown in Fig.
8
.
Observe that, while the formulas of
mCPL
have no free-names, Boolean formulas
do
have names, and rules do depend on them. Notably, the Flipcoin Rule intuitively tells that one can construct a new proof of
A
from two proofs of the same formula by flipping a (named) coin.

In Fig.
9
we illustrate how any derivation of
mCPL
can be decorated with terms from
\Lambda_{\nu}
.
To any formula
A
of
mCPL
, we can straightforwardly associate a type
A^{\bullet}
, and to any multiset
\Gamma=A_{1},\dots,A_{n}
of formulas of
mCPL
we associate the context
\Gamma^{\bullet}=\{x_{1}:A_{1}^{\bullet},\dots,x_{n}:A_{n}^{\bullet}\}
. Using the decorations in Fig.
9
we deduce then:

Proposition 68
.

To any
mCPL
-derivation
D
of
\Gamma\vdash^{X}b\rightarrowtail\mathbf{C}^{r}A_{0}
is associated a term
{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}t}_{D}
such that
\Gamma^{\bullet}\vdash^{X,r}{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}t}_{D}:b\rightarrowtail(A_{0})^{\bullet}
.

Observe that from this fact, through the embedding of
\lambda\textsf{{CPL}}
inside
CPL
, we deduce an embedding of
mCPL
inside
CPL
:

Corollary 7

(embedding of
mCPL
inside
CPL
)
.

If
\Delta\vdash_{\textsf{{mCPL}}}^{X}b\rightarrowtail A
then
\vdash_{\textsf{{CPL}}}^{X}b\rightarrowtail A^{\bullet*},\bot\leftarrowtail\Delta^{\bullet*}
.

Initial Sequents

\mathsf{R}_{\bot}

\Delta\vdash^{X}\bot\rightarrowtail A

\mathrm{FN}(b)\subseteq X

{\mathbf{Ax}}

\Gamma,A\vdash^{X}b\rightarrowtail A

Logical Rules

\Gamma,A\vdash^{X}b\rightarrowtail\mathbf{C}^{q}B_{0}

\mathsf{R}_{\Rightarrow}^{\text{E}}

\Gamma\vdash^{X}b\rightarrowtail\mathbf{C}^{q}(A\Rightarrow B_{0})

\Gamma\vdash^{X}c\rightarrowtail\mathbf{C}^{r}(A\Rightarrow B_{0})

\Gamma\vdash^{X}d\rightarrowtail A

b\vDash c\land d

\mathsf{R}_{\Rightarrow}^{\text{I}}

\Gamma\vdash^{X}b\rightarrowtail\mathbf{C}^{r}B_{0}

Flipcoin Rule

\Gamma,A\vdash^{X\cup\{a\}}c\rightarrowtail A

\Gamma\vdash^{X\cup\{a\}}d\rightarrowtail A

b\vDash^{X\cup\{a\}}(c\land x_{i}^{a})\vee(d\land\lnot x_{i}^{a})

\mathsf{R}_{\vee}

\Gamma\vdash^{X\cup\{a\}}b\rightarrowtail A

Counting Rule

\Gamma\vdash^{X\cup\{a\}}c\rightarrowtail\mathbf{C}^{q}A_{0}

\left(\llbracket c_{i}\rrbracket_{\{a\}})\geq r\right)_{i\leq k}

b\vDash\bigvee_{i}^{k}d_{i}

\mathsf{R}_{\mathbf{C}}

\Gamma\vdash^{X}b\rightarrowtail\mathbf{C}^{q\cdot r}A_{0}

(where
\bigvee_{i}c_{i}\land d_{i}
is a weak
a
-decomposition of
c
)

Figure 8
:
Rules of
mCPL
.

Initial Sequents

\mathsf{R}_{\bot}

\Delta\vdash^{X}{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\Omega}:\bot\rightarrowtail A

\mathrm{FN}(b)\subseteq X

{\mathbf{Ax}}

\Gamma,{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}x}:A\vdash^{X}{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}x}:b\rightarrowtail A

Logical Rules

\Gamma,{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}x}:A\vdash^{X}{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}t}:b\rightarrowtail\mathbf{C}^{q}B_{0}

\mathsf{R}_{\Rightarrow}^{\text{E}}

\Gamma\vdash^{X}{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\lambda x.t}:b\rightarrowtail\mathbf{C}^{q}(A\Rightarrow B_{0})

\Gamma\vdash^{X}{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}t}:c\rightarrowtail\mathbf{C}^{r}(A\Rightarrow B_{0})

\Gamma\vdash^{X}{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}u}:d\rightarrowtail A

b\vDash c\land d

\mathsf{R}_{\Rightarrow}^{\text{I}}

\Gamma\vdash^{X}{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}tu}:b\rightarrowtail\mathbf{C}^{r}B_{0}

Flipcoin Rule

\Gamma,A\vdash^{X\cup\{a\}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}t}:c\rightarrowtail A

\Gamma\vdash^{X\cup\{a\}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}u}:d\rightarrowtail A

b\vDash^{X\cup\{a\}}(c\land x_{i}^{a})\vee(d\land\lnot x_{i}^{a})

\mathsf{R}_{\vee}

\Gamma\vdash^{X\cup\{a\}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}t\oplus_{a}^{i}u}:b\rightarrowtail A

Counting Rule

\Gamma\vdash^{X\cup\{a\}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}t}:c\rightarrowtail\mathbf{C}^{q}A_{0}

\left(\llbracket c_{i}\rrbracket_{\{a\}})\geq r\right)_{i\leq k}

b\vDash\bigvee_{i}^{k}d_{i}

\mathsf{R}_{\mathbf{C}}

\Gamma\vdash^{X}{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\nu a.t}:b\rightarrowtail\mathbf{C}^{q\cdot r}A_{0}

(where
\bigvee_{i}c_{i}\land d_{i}
is a weak
a
-decomposition of
c
)

:
Rules of
mCPL
decorated with terms.
